=== eoka usage
Usage: eoka [OPTIONS] [COMMAND]
=== eoka help
Browser automation CLI

Usage: eoka [OPTIONS] [COMMAND]

Commands:
  view                Compact agent page state with scoped control references
  step                Perform one agent action and return its outcome and fresh page state
  action              Submit a typed action with an operation ID
  operations          Inspect durable action records
  open                Navigate to URL
  back                Go back in history
  forward             Go forward in history
  reload              Reload current page
  snapshot            Accessibility tree with @eN refs
  observe             Inspect page context and interactive elements with indices
  screenshot          Take screenshot
  recording           Record the current tab to an MP4 file
  captcha             Handle CAPTCHAs locally or solve/inject provider tokens
  emulate             Emulate a device viewport
  info                Get page URL and title
  text                Get all visible text on page
  find                Find elements by text substring
  content             Get the page HTML
  dialog              Accept or dismiss an open alert, confirm or prompt dialog
  get-text            Get the text of the first element matching a CSS selector
  get-attribute       Get an attribute of the first element matching a CSS selector
  exists              Check whether a CSS selector matches an element
  click-shadow        Click an element inside open or closed shadow roots
  click               Click element
  dblclick            Double-click element
  fill                Clear and fill input
  select              Select dropdown option
  hover               Hover over element
  key                 Press keyboard key
  mouse-down          Press and hold a mouse button at viewport coordinates
  mouse-move          Move the mouse to viewport coordinates
  mouse-up            Release a held mouse button at viewport coordinates
  key-down            Press and hold a keyboard key
  key-up              Release a held keyboard key
  release-all-inputs  Release every held mouse button and key
  scroll              Scroll page or element into view
  eval                Execute JavaScript and return result
  frames              List frame IDs, URLs and names in the active page
  frame-eval          Execute JavaScript in an isolated frame world
  exec                Execute JavaScript without returning a value
  tack                Run Tack TypeScript against the active eoka browser session
  tools               Inspect the generated eoka tool catalog
  download            Download files through the existing browser session
  fetch               Fetch bounded text from browser context; use download for files
  network             Network recording, inspection, export, and interception
  cookies             Get all cookies
  set-cookie          Set a cookie
  delete-cookie       Delete a cookie by name
  clear-cookies       Clear all cookies
  storage             Get localStorage
  set-storage         Set storage value
  dump-storage        Dump localStorage and sessionStorage
  save-state          Save cookies and storage to JSON
  load-state          Load cookies and storage from JSON
  headers             Set persistent extra HTTP headers
  console             Read browser console output
  errors              Read JavaScript errors
  tab                 Tab management
  wait                Wait for time, text, URL, selector, or load state
  batch               Execute multiple commands in sequence
  fake-camera         Inject fake camera from a video file
  wasm                WASM linear memory operations
  js                  Manage the per-domain JavaScript policy
  spa-info            Detect SPA router type
  spa-navigate        Navigate SPA without page reload
  sessions            List all sessions
  status              Show whether the session is running, what it is running and what blocks its tab
  doctor              Diagnose local eoka runtime state
  kill                Force-kill daemon
  update              Update this eoka binary and the installed agent skill from the latest GitHub release [alias: upgrade]
  skill               Install or refresh the agent skill this binary ships
  close               Close browser and daemon
  cdp-url             Print the discovered DevTools WebSocket URL
  clone-from          Snapshot cookies and storage from a running Chrome
  help                Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')
  -V, --version
          Print version

=== eoka long help
Browser automation CLI

Usage: eoka [OPTIONS] [COMMAND]

Commands:
  view                Compact agent page state with scoped control references
  step                Perform one agent action and return its outcome and fresh page state
  action              Submit a typed action with an operation ID
  operations          Inspect durable action records
  open                Navigate to URL
  back                Go back in history
  forward             Go forward in history
  reload              Reload current page
  snapshot            Accessibility tree with @eN refs
  observe             Inspect page context and interactive elements with indices
  screenshot          Take screenshot
  recording           Record the current tab to an MP4 file
  captcha             Handle CAPTCHAs locally or solve/inject provider tokens
  emulate             Emulate a device viewport
  info                Get page URL and title
  text                Get all visible text on page
  find                Find elements by text substring
  content             Get the page HTML
  dialog              Accept or dismiss an open alert, confirm or prompt dialog
  get-text            Get the text of the first element matching a CSS selector
  get-attribute       Get an attribute of the first element matching a CSS selector
  exists              Check whether a CSS selector matches an element
  click-shadow        Click an element inside open or closed shadow roots
  click               Click element
  dblclick            Double-click element
  fill                Clear and fill input
  select              Select dropdown option
  hover               Hover over element
  key                 Press keyboard key
  mouse-down          Press and hold a mouse button at viewport coordinates
  mouse-move          Move the mouse to viewport coordinates
  mouse-up            Release a held mouse button at viewport coordinates
  key-down            Press and hold a keyboard key
  key-up              Release a held keyboard key
  release-all-inputs  Release every held mouse button and key
  scroll              Scroll page or element into view
  eval                Execute JavaScript and return result
  frames              List frame IDs, URLs and names in the active page
  frame-eval          Execute JavaScript in an isolated frame world
  exec                Execute JavaScript without returning a value
  tack                Run Tack TypeScript against the active eoka browser session
  tools               Inspect the generated eoka tool catalog
  download            Download files through the existing browser session
  fetch               Fetch bounded text from browser context; use download for files
  network             Network recording, inspection, export, and interception
  cookies             Get all cookies
  set-cookie          Set a cookie
  delete-cookie       Delete a cookie by name
  clear-cookies       Clear all cookies
  storage             Get localStorage
  set-storage         Set storage value
  dump-storage        Dump localStorage and sessionStorage
  save-state          Save cookies and storage to JSON
  load-state          Load cookies and storage from JSON
  headers             Set persistent extra HTTP headers
  console             Read browser console output
  errors              Read JavaScript errors
  tab                 Tab management
  wait                Wait for time, text, URL, selector, or load state
  batch               Execute multiple commands in sequence
  fake-camera         Inject fake camera from a video file
  wasm                WASM linear memory operations
  js                  Manage the per-domain JavaScript policy
  spa-info            Detect SPA router type
  spa-navigate        Navigate SPA without page reload
  sessions            List all sessions
  status              Show whether the session is running, what it is running and what blocks its tab
  doctor              Diagnose local eoka runtime state
  kill                Force-kill daemon
  update              Update this eoka binary and the installed agent skill from the latest GitHub release [alias: upgrade]
  skill               Install or refresh the agent skill this binary ships
  close               Close browser and daemon
  cdp-url             Print the discovered DevTools WebSocket URL
  clone-from          Snapshot cookies and storage from a running Chrome
  help                Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

  -V, --version
          Print version

Deadlines:
  Every command returns within a bounded time and never replays an action.
  A failure carries error_detail.code and a hint naming the next command:
    dialog_open          a JavaScript dialog blocks the tab: dialog accept | dialog dismiss
    navigation_pending   the tab waits for a server: wait --url, or reload | open URL to abandon it
    navigation_timeout   no response before the deadline; the load was stopped and the tab kept its page
    daemon_busy          another command is running; this one was not started (EOKA_QUEUE_WAIT_MS, default 5000)
    page_unresponsive    the page's main thread is frozen: retry, or tab new URL
    reply_timeout        the daemon did not reply in time (EOKA_REPLY_TIMEOUT_MS overrides the default)
    daemon_unresponsive  the daemon did not answer its handshake within 5 s
  Page reads answer within 15 s, view within --timeout-ms (3000), open and actions
  within --action-timeout-ms (30000). status shows what a session is running.

=== eoka view usage
Usage: eoka view [OPTIONS]
=== eoka view help
Compact agent page state with scoped control references

Usage: eoka view [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --cursor <CURSOR>
          Continue from a cursor returned by a previous view
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --role <ROLE>
          Only controls with this role
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --name <NAME>
          Only controls whose accessible name contains this text
      --max-controls <MAX_CONTROLS>
          Maximum controls to return, 1 through 200 [default: 40]
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --timeout-ms <TIMEOUT_MS>
          Observation deadline, 50 through 60000 ms [default: 3000]
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka view long help
Compact agent page state with scoped control references.

Answers within --timeout-ms. When the tab cannot answer, it fails at once with the reason and the next command: dialog_open, navigation_pending, page_unresponsive, or daemon_busy while another command is running.

Usage: eoka view [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --cursor <CURSOR>
          Continue from a cursor returned by a previous view

      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --role <ROLE>
          Only controls with this role

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --name <NAME>
          Only controls whose accessible name contains this text

      --max-controls <MAX_CONTROLS>
          Maximum controls to return, 1 through 200
          
          [default: 40]

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --timeout-ms <TIMEOUT_MS>
          Observation deadline, 50 through 60000 ms
          
          [default: 3000]

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

          Start with JavaScript blocked by default

          Domain to always block JavaScript on

          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

=== eoka step long help
Perform one agent action and return its outcome and fresh page state.

REQUEST is JSON. Steps verify by default: give clicks and keys an explicit expected result. Exploratory input needs "mode": "explore" and exits 4 when unverified. Fill is sequential native input unless "input_mode": "bulk".

Usage: eoka step [OPTIONS] <REQUEST>

Arguments:
  <REQUEST>
          Step request as JSON

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts

=== eoka open usage
Usage: eoka open [OPTIONS] <URL>
=== eoka open help
Navigate to URL

Usage: eoka open [OPTIONS] <URL>

Arguments:
  <URL>  URL, or a path relative to the current page

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --headers <HEADERS>
          Extra HTTP headers as a JSON object
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --user-agent <USER_AGENT>
          User-Agent header for this navigation
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --bypass-csp
          Disable the page's Content-Security-Policy
      --inject-js <FILE>
          JavaScript source, or a .js file, to run on each new document before page scripts
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --load-state <FILE>
          Restore a save-state file before navigating
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka open long help
Navigate to URL. file: URLs are blocked unless EOKA_ALLOW_FILE_URLS=1 was set when the daemon started.

A load still pending from an earlier command is replaced. If the server sends no response before --action-timeout-ms (default 30000), the load is stopped, the tab keeps the page it had, and the command fails with navigation_timeout.

Usage: eoka open [OPTIONS] <URL>

Arguments:
  <URL>
          URL, or a path relative to the current page

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --headers <HEADERS>
          Extra HTTP headers as a JSON object

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --user-agent <USER_AGENT>
          User-Agent header for this navigation

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --bypass-csp
          Disable the page's Content-Security-Policy

      --inject-js <FILE>
          JavaScript source, or a .js file, to run on each new document before page scripts

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --load-state <FILE>
          Restore a save-state file before navigating

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

      --max <MAX>
          Maximum elements to return
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>

      --structured

      --headless

Inspect page context and interactive elements with indices

Usage: eoka observe [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --filter <FILTER>
          Filter elements: inputs, buttons, or all

      --max <MAX>
          Maximum elements to return

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>

          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --structured

      --no-stealth

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

=== eoka captcha usage
Usage: eoka captcha [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>
=== eoka captcha help
Handle CAPTCHAs locally or solve/inject provider tokens

Usage: eoka captcha [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>

Commands:
  detect     Detect an hCaptcha or reCAPTCHA widget and report its type and site key
  datadome   Attempt local DataDome clearance on the current tab; keep the session open
  turnstile  Click the Cloudflare Turnstile checkbox on the current tab until the challenge clears
  solve      Solve a CAPTCHA through the Anti-Captcha provider
  inject     Inject a solved token into the current page
  help       Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>

      --proxy-file <FILE>

=== eoka captcha datadome long help
Attempt local DataDome clearance on the current tab. No API key needed.

Requires an existing session; foregrounds the tab and leaves it open. challenge_cleared is not proof of login; not_present is a no-op; every other outcome exits nonzero. An old daemon is not restarted automatically.

Usage: eoka captcha datadome [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --max-attempts <MAX_ATTEMPTS>
          Clearance attempts, 1 through 4
          
          [default: 1]

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --timeout-ms <TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total budget, 5000 through 120000 ms
          
          [default: 60000]

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect

          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka captcha turnstile long help
Click the Cloudflare Turnstile checkbox on the current tab until it leaves the challenge page.

Reports clicks and elapsed_ms; a normal page returns zero clicks. --auto-solve-cloudflare does this for every tab of a browser eoka launches.

Usage: eoka captcha turnstile [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --timeout-ms <TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total budget, at most 120000 ms
          
          [default: 30000]

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

=== eoka captcha solve usage
Usage: eoka captcha solve [OPTIONS] --captcha-type <CAPTCHA_TYPE> --website-url <WEBSITE_URL> --website-key <WEBSITE_KEY>
=== eoka captcha solve help
Solve a CAPTCHA through the Anti-Captcha provider

Usage: eoka captcha solve [OPTIONS] --captcha-type <CAPTCHA_TYPE> --website-url <WEBSITE_URL> --website-key <WEBSITE_KEY>

Options:
      --captcha-type <CAPTCHA_TYPE>
          recaptcha_v2, recaptcha_v2_enterprise, recaptcha_v3, hcaptcha or amazon_waf
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --website-url <WEBSITE_URL>
          URL of the page showing the CAPTCHA
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --website-key <WEBSITE_KEY>
          Site key from the page (amazon_waf: key from window.gokuProps)
      --api-key <API_KEY>
          Anti-Captcha API key [env: ANTI_CAPTCHA_KEY=]
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --page-action <PAGE_ACTION>
          recaptcha_v3 page action [default: submit]
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --min-score <MIN_SCORE>
          recaptcha_v3 minimum score [default: 0.3]
      --enterprise-payload <ENTERPRISE_PAYLOAD>
          recaptcha_v2_enterprise extra render parameters as JSON, such as {"s": ...}
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --api-domain <API_DOMAIN>
          recaptcha_v2_enterprise widget domain, such as www.google.com
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --iv <IV>
          amazon_waf iv from window.gokuProps (required)
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --context <CONTEXT>
          amazon_waf context from window.gokuProps (required)
      --captcha-script <CAPTCHA_SCRIPT>
          amazon_waf captcha script URL
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --challenge-script <CHALLENGE_SCRIPT>
          amazon_waf challenge script URL
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --inject
          Inject the token into the current page after solving
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --inject-callback <INJECT_CALLBACK>
          Page callback to call with the injected token
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --click-after <CLICK_AFTER>
          Target to click after injecting
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka captcha help help
Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Usage: eoka captcha help [COMMAND]

Commands:
  detect     Detect an hCaptcha or reCAPTCHA widget and report its type and site key
  datadome   Attempt local DataDome clearance on the current tab; keep the session open
  turnstile  Click the Cloudflare Turnstile checkbox on the current tab until the challenge clears
  solve      Solve a CAPTCHA through the Anti-Captcha provider
  inject     Inject a solved token into the current page
  help       Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

=== eoka dialog long help
Accept or dismiss an open alert, confirm, prompt or beforeunload dialog.

Chrome answers no page command while a dialog is open, so other commands fail with dialog_open until it is handled. These two commands also work while another command is still running.

Usage: eoka dialog [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>

Commands:
  accept   Accept the open dialog
  dismiss  Dismiss the open dialog
  help     Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:

          Expected action result as typed JSON

=== eoka click usage
Usage: eoka click [OPTIONS] <TARGET>
=== eoka click help
Click element

Usage: eoka click [OPTIONS] <TARGET>

Arguments:
  <TARGET>  text:Submit (or bare text), placeholder:Email, css:#id, id:login, role:button, or a target from view

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka click long help
Click element. Requires --expect with the result that proves the click worked; without it the command fails before any input.

Usage: eoka click [OPTIONS] <TARGET>

Arguments:
  <TARGET>
          text:Submit (or bare text), placeholder:Email, css:#id, id:login, role:button, or a target from view

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>

          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare

          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka key long help
Press a key on the current focus, including a focused frame.

`o` sends a native printable key; `Shift+o` sends `O`. Shift uses US-keyboard ASCII mappings; Ctrl/Alt/Meta shortcuts do not insert text. Requires --expect: choose an expectation that proves the intended effect, because read-only controls and prevented events can reject edits.

Usage: eoka key [OPTIONS] <KEY>

Arguments:
  <KEY>
          Key name or chord such as Enter, a, Shift+o, Control+a

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help

Usage: eoka eval [OPTIONS] [CODE]

=== eoka eval long help
Execute a JavaScript function and return its JSON result.

CODE is a function, also when loaded with --file. It runs once in a fresh scope, promises are awaited and exceptions fail the command. The result must be JSON: undefined, cycles, non-finite numbers and non-JSON objects are rejected. Source, arguments and result are each bounded to 1 MiB. --raw instead evaluates source in global scope and returns display text, not typed JSON.

Usage: eoka eval [OPTIONS] [CODE]

Arguments:
  [CODE]
          JavaScript source; omit to use --file

Options:
      --args <ARGS>
          JSON array of arguments passed to the function
          
          [default: []]

      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --raw
          Treat CODE as an expression, not a function

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

  -f, --file <FILE>
          Read JavaScript from a file

      --no-return
          Run without returning a result

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --max-size <MAX_SIZE>
          Fail when the JSON result exceeds this many bytes (at most 1 MiB)

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --no-await
          Do not await a returned promise; requires --raw

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

=== eoka frame-eval long help
Execute JavaScript in an isolated frame world, including cross-origin OOPIFs.

The code has DOM access but not the page's own JavaScript globals. Requires an existing session; never launches, restarts or replays. Frame IDs change on navigation: list them again with `frames`.

Usage: eoka frame-eval [OPTIONS] <FRAME> [CODE]

Arguments:
  <FRAME>
          CSS selector matching one frame, or id:<frame-id> from frames; no bare URL/index

  [CODE]
          JavaScript source; omit to use --file

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

  -f, --file <FILE>
          Read JavaScript from a file

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

          JSON output mode

=== eoka download usage
Usage: eoka download [OPTIONS] --out <OUTPUT> <--click <CLICK>|--url <URL>>
       eoka download <COMMAND>
=== eoka download help
Download files through the existing browser session

Usage: eoka download [OPTIONS] --out <OUTPUT> <--click <CLICK>|--url <URL>>
       eoka download <COMMAND>

Commands:
  list    List download jobs in this session
  status  Show one job
  wait    Wait for a job; a timeout leaves it running
  cancel  Cancel a job
  help    Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --click <CLICK>
          Existing click target: @e1, text:Download, or css:button
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --url <URL>
          Direct HTTP(S) attachment URL
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --out <OUTPUT>
          New output filename; parent must exist
      --expect-bytes <EXPECT_BYTES>
          Fail unless the file has exactly this many bytes
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --start-timeout-ms <START_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Deadline for the native download to start, at most 300000 [default: 30000]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --wait
          Wait for this job without blocking other commands
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --timeout-ms <TIMEOUT_MS>
          How long --wait waits; the job keeps running after it [default: 30000]
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka download long help
Download files through the existing eoka-owned browser session; borrowed and remote browsers are rejected.

Starting returns a job ID. One file runs per session. Start actions are never replayed. Closing the session cancels unfinished jobs; published files survive. History persists across daemon restarts under EOKA_STATE_DIR; partial transfers are not resumed and clicks are never replayed. Completion means a verified regular file was atomically published without replacing anything; on failure inspect published_path and staging_path. Relative --out paths resolve in the calling shell's directory.

Usage: eoka download [OPTIONS] --out <OUTPUT> <--click <CLICK>|--url <URL>>
       eoka download <COMMAND>

Commands:
  list    List download jobs in this session
  status  Show one job
  wait    Wait for a job; a timeout leaves it running
  cancel  Cancel a job
  help    Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --click <CLICK>
          Existing click target: @e1, text:Download, or css:button

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --url <URL>
          Direct HTTP(S) attachment URL

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --out <OUTPUT>
          New output filename; parent must exist

      --expect-bytes <EXPECT_BYTES>
          Fail unless the file has exactly this many bytes

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --start-timeout-ms <START_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Deadline for the native download to start, at most 300000
          
          [default: 30000]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --wait
          Wait for this job without blocking other commands

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --timeout-ms <TIMEOUT_MS>
          How long --wait waits; the job keeps running after it
          
          [default: 30000]

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

=== eoka fetch usage
Usage: eoka fetch [OPTIONS] <URL>
=== eoka fetch help
Fetch bounded text from browser context; use download for files

Usage: eoka fetch [OPTIONS] <URL>

Arguments:
  <URL>  URL to fetch from the page's context

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
  -m, --method <METHOD>
          HTTP method [default: GET]
      --headers <HEADERS>
          Request headers as a JSON object
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
  -b, --body <BODY>
          Request body
      --redirect <REDIRECT>
          Fetch redirect mode: follow, error or manual
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --body-only
          Print only the body; fails on binary or truncated bodies
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --max-body <MAX_BODY>
          Raw text bytes to retain: default 8192, maximum 16777216; 0 means headers only
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka fetch long help
Fetch from inside the page with its cookies and fingerprint; use download for files.

JSON output reports bytesRead, truncated and bodyOmitted. Known binary bodies are skipped.

Usage: eoka fetch [OPTIONS] <URL>

Arguments:
  <URL>
          URL to fetch from the page's context

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

  -m, --method <METHOD>
          HTTP method [default: GET]

      --headers <HEADERS>
          Request headers as a JSON object

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

  -b, --body <BODY>
          Request body

      --redirect <REDIRECT>
          Fetch redirect mode: follow, error or manual

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --body-only
          Print only the body; fails on binary or truncated bodies

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --max-body <MAX_BODY>
          Raw text bytes to retain: default 8192, maximum 16777216; 0 means headers only

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

=== eoka network usage
Usage: eoka network [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>
=== eoka network help
Network recording, inspection, export, and interception

Usage: eoka network [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>

Commands:
  record     Start, stop or inspect network recording
  log        List recorded requests
  show       Show one recorded request
  har        Write recorded requests as a HAR file
  export     Export recorded requests as HAR or JSON
  wait       Wait for a matching request
  clear      Discard recorded requests
  intercept  Capture or mock requests matching a URL pattern
  help       Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka network record long help
Start, stop or inspect network recording.

Bodies are captured unredacted by default; treat logs and exports as sensitive.

Usage: eoka network record [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>

Commands:
  start   Start recording
  stop    Stop recording
  status  Show recording state
  help    Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

Usage: eoka network log [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --limit <LIMIT>
          Maximum entries to return
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --pattern <PATTERN>
          URL glob such as */api/*
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --method <METHOD>
          Only this HTTP method
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --status <STATUS>
          Only this response status
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --since <SINCE>
          Only entries after this request ID
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --compact
          One line per request

          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless

          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help
