=== eoka network intercept long help
Capture or mock requests matching a URL pattern.

The log keeps the latest 1000 entries. Response and capture files must be regular files of at most 8 MiB; FIFOs and devices are rejected, and each file operation has a 500 ms deadline. A failed capture, read or fulfillment invalidates DataDome clearance. Requests that may already have been dispatched are never replayed.

Usage: eoka network intercept [OPTIONS] <COMMAND>

Commands:
  add     Add an interception rule
  list    List interception rules
  remove  Remove an interception rule
  log     Read the interception log (latest 1000 entries)
  help    Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

=== eoka network intercept add help
Add an interception rule

Usage: eoka network intercept add [OPTIONS] <URL_PATTERN>

Arguments:
  <URL_PATTERN>  URL glob such as */api/data*

Options:
      --capture <CAPTURE>
          Write matching requests to this file
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --respond <RESPOND>
          Serve this file's exact bytes as the response
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --status <STATUS>
          Status code for --respond [default: 200]
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless

Read the interception log (latest 1000 entries)

Usage: eoka network intercept log [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --clear
          Clear the log after reading
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka network help help
Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Usage: eoka network help [COMMAND]

Commands:
  record     Start, stop or inspect network recording
  log        List recorded requests
  show       Show one recorded request
  har        Write recorded requests as a HAR file
  export     Export recorded requests as HAR or JSON
  wait       Wait for a matching request
  clear      Discard recorded requests
  intercept  Capture or mock requests matching a URL pattern
  help       Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

=== eoka save-state long help
Save the current page's cookies and storage to JSON.

The path must end in .json. The file is written atomically, owner-only, and can only replace an empty file or an earlier state file.

Usage: eoka save-state [OPTIONS] <PATH>

Arguments:
  <PATH>
          State file to write; must end in .json

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka load-state long help
Load cookies and storage from a save-state file.

Cookies are replaced only on the domains the file covers, including their parent domains and subdomains; every other site's cookies are kept. Storage is restored only on the saved origin, and skipped keys are reported.

Usage: eoka load-state [OPTIONS] <PATH>

Arguments:
  <PATH>
          State file written by save-state

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --no-navigate
          Do not navigate to the saved URL first

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

=== eoka tab new help
Open a tab, optionally at a URL

Usage: eoka tab new [OPTIONS] [URL]

Arguments:
  [URL]  URL to open [default: blank page]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka wait usage
Usage: eoka wait [OPTIONS] [MS]
=== eoka wait help
Wait for time, text, URL, selector, or load state

Usage: eoka wait [OPTIONS] [MS]

Arguments:
  [MS]  Fixed delay in milliseconds

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --text <TEXT>
          Wait until this text is visible
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --url <URL>
          Wait until the URL contains this string
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --load <LOAD>
          Wait for network idle
      --selector <SELECTOR>
          Wait until this CSS selector reaches --state
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --state <STATE>
          Selector condition [default: visible] [possible values: attached, visible, hidden]
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
  -t, --timeout <TIMEOUT>
          Timeout in milliseconds
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help (see more with '--help')

=== eoka wait long help
Wait for time, text, URL, selector, or load state.

A condition wait returns by --timeout (default 10000 ms). It keeps waiting through a navigation that has not answered yet and reports navigation_pending if that is why it timed out.

Usage: eoka wait [OPTIONS] [MS]

Arguments:
  [MS]
          Fixed delay in milliseconds

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --text <TEXT>
          Wait until this text is visible

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --url <URL>
          Wait until the URL contains this string

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --load <LOAD>
          Wait for network idle

      --selector <SELECTOR>
          Wait until this CSS selector reaches --state

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --state <STATE>
          Selector condition [default: visible]
          
          [possible values: attached, visible, hidden]

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

  -t, --timeout <TIMEOUT>
          Timeout in milliseconds

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

=== eoka wasm find help
Search memory for a hex byte pattern

Usage: eoka wasm find [OPTIONS] <PATTERN>

Arguments:
  <PATTERN>  Hex bytes to find; spaces allowed, such as "ff d8 ff e0"

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --start <START>
          Start address [default: 0]
      --end <END>
          End address [default: end of memory]
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --max <MAX>
          Maximum matches [default: 20]
      --memory <MEMORY>
          JavaScript expression for the WebAssembly.Memory [default: auto-detect]
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help

Usage: eoka js block [OPTIONS] <DOMAIN>

Arguments:
  <DOMAIN>  Domain such as example.com

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON
      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action
      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds
      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances [default: default]
      --json
          JSON output mode
      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers
      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session
      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session
      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL [env: EOKA_CDP=]
      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229 [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]
      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome
      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]
      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]
      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]
      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default) [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]
      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it) [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]
      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]
      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser [env: EOKA_PROXY=]
      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]
      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]
      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on
      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on
  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka js block long help
Always block JavaScript on a domain

Usage: eoka js block [OPTIONS] <DOMAIN>

Arguments:
  <DOMAIN>
          Domain such as example.com

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka js help usage
Usage: eoka js help [COMMAND]
=== eoka js help help
Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Usage: eoka js help [COMMAND]

Commands:
  mode    Set the default policy
  allow   Always run JavaScript on a domain
  block   Always block JavaScript on a domain
  remove  Remove a domain rule
  list    List the policy
  help    Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

=== eoka js help long help
Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Commands:
  mode    Set the default policy
  allow   Always run JavaScript on a domain
  block   Always block JavaScript on a domain
  remove  Remove a domain rule
  list    List the policy
  help    Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

=== eoka status long help
Show whether the session is running, what it is running and what blocks its tab.

Answers even while another command runs. Reports the running command and its age (busy), queued commands, and an open dialog or pending navigation (blocker) with the command that clears it. A daemon that accepts connections but does not answer is reported as not responsive.

Usage: eoka status [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

=== eoka kill long help
Force-kill the session daemon and its browser. Page state is lost.

A last resort for a daemon that status reports as not responsive. A busy or blocked session is recovered with the command its error names instead.

Usage: eoka kill [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>

=== eoka update long help
Update this eoka binary and the installed agent skill from the latest GitHub release.

Downloads the release archive for this platform, verifies it against the release SHA256SUMS and replaces the running executable. Installed copies of the agent skill are then rewritten to match the installed binary, also when the binary was already current; --keep-skill leaves them alone. Session daemons keep running their old version until restarted; they are listed, and stopped only with --kill-daemons.

Usage: eoka update [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --check
          Report the installed and latest versions without installing

      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --force
          Reinstall even when already on the latest release

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

      --kill-daemons
          Stop session daemons still running another version (closes their browsers)

      --keep-skill
          Leave installed copies of the agent skill as they are

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>

          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth

          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)

          Print help (see a summary with '-h')

      --session <SESSION>
          Session name for isolated browser instances
          
          [default: default]

      --json
          JSON output mode

      --agent
          JSON output plus structured metadata for agent callers

      --headed
          Create a headed browser or require an existing headed session

      --headless
          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka skill refresh long help
Rewrite installed copies of the agent skill to match this binary.

Looks for an eoka skill under ~/.agents/skills, ~/.claude/skills, ~/.codex/skills, ~/.pi/agent/skills, ~/.config/opencode/skills, ~/.cursor/skills and ~/.gemini/skills, and under $CODEX_HOME/skills and $CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/skills when those are set. Only files that differ are rewritten; other files in the directory are left alone. Project-local copies are not searched: name one with `eoka skill install DIR`. `eoka update` runs this after installing a release.

Usage: eoka skill refresh [OPTIONS]

Options:
      --expect <EXPECT>
          Expected action result as typed JSON

      --operation-id <OPERATION_ID>
          Stable UUID for a consequential action

      --action-timeout-ms <ACTION_TIMEOUT_MS>
          Total action deadline in milliseconds

          [default: default]

      --auto-connect

          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

          Create a headless browser or require an existing headless session

      --cdp <PORT|URL>
          Connect to Chrome by DevTools port or WebSocket URL
          
          [env: EOKA_CDP=]

      --auto-connect
          Auto-discover a running Chrome on ports 9222-9229
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_CONNECT=]

      --clone-state-from <PORT|URL>
          Launch fresh Chrome and pre-load cookies/storage from a running Chrome

      --from-profile <auto|PATH>
          Launch with a copy of an existing Chrome profile directory
          
          [env: EOKA_FROM_PROFILE=]

      --no-stealth
          Disable stealth CDP filtering and evasion script injection
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_STEALTH=]

      --persist
          Keep a durable Chrome profile for this session across restarts
          
          [env: EOKA_PERSIST=]

      --no-geo-align
          Disable IP-geolocation timezone/language alignment (on by default)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_GEO_ALIGN=]

      --no-sandbox
          Disable Chrome's sandbox (only for containers/CI that cannot run it)
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_SANDBOX=]

      --auto-solve-cloudflare
          Solve full-page Cloudflare challenges in every tab automatically
          
          [env: EOKA_AUTO_SOLVE_CLOUDFLARE=]

      --proxy <URL>
          Proxy URL for launched browser
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY=]

      --proxy-file <FILE>
          Read proxy URL from a file
          
          [env: EOKA_PROXY_FILE=]

      --no-js
          Start with JavaScript blocked by default
          
          [env: EOKA_NO_JS=]

      --js-allow <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always run JavaScript on

      --js-block <DOMAIN>
          Domain to always block JavaScript on

  -h, --help
          Print help

=== eoka help usage
Usage: eoka help [COMMAND]
=== eoka help help
Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

Usage: eoka help [COMMAND]

Commands:
  view                Compact agent page state with scoped control references
  step                Perform one agent action and return its outcome and fresh page state
  action              Submit a typed action with an operation ID
  operations          Inspect durable action records
  open                Navigate to URL
  back                Go back in history
  forward             Go forward in history
  reload              Reload current page
  snapshot            Accessibility tree with @eN refs
  observe             Inspect page context and interactive elements with indices
  screenshot          Take screenshot
  recording           Record the current tab to an MP4 file
  captcha             Handle CAPTCHAs locally or solve/inject provider tokens
  emulate             Emulate a device viewport
  info                Get page URL and title
  text                Get all visible text on page
  find                Find elements by text substring
  content             Get the page HTML
  dialog              Accept or dismiss an open alert, confirm or prompt dialog
  get-text            Get the text of the first element matching a CSS selector
  get-attribute       Get an attribute of the first element matching a CSS selector
  exists              Check whether a CSS selector matches an element
  click-shadow        Click an element inside open or closed shadow roots
  click               Click element
  dblclick            Double-click element
  fill                Clear and fill input
  select              Select dropdown option
  hover               Hover over element
  key                 Press keyboard key
  mouse-down          Press and hold a mouse button at viewport coordinates
  mouse-move          Move the mouse to viewport coordinates
  mouse-up            Release a held mouse button at viewport coordinates
  key-down            Press and hold a keyboard key
  key-up              Release a held keyboard key
  release-all-inputs  Release every held mouse button and key
  scroll              Scroll page or element into view
  eval                Execute JavaScript and return result
  frames              List frame IDs, URLs and names in the active page
  frame-eval          Execute JavaScript in an isolated frame world
  exec                Execute JavaScript without returning a value
  tack                Run Tack TypeScript against the active eoka browser session
  tools               Inspect the generated eoka tool catalog
  download            Download files through the existing browser session
  fetch               Fetch bounded text from browser context; use download for files
  network             Network recording, inspection, export, and interception
  cookies             Get all cookies
  set-cookie          Set a cookie
  delete-cookie       Delete a cookie by name
  clear-cookies       Clear all cookies
  storage             Get localStorage
  set-storage         Set storage value
  dump-storage        Dump localStorage and sessionStorage
  save-state          Save cookies and storage to JSON
  load-state          Load cookies and storage from JSON
  headers             Set persistent extra HTTP headers
  console             Read browser console output
  errors              Read JavaScript errors
  tab                 Tab management
  wait                Wait for time, text, URL, selector, or load state
  batch               Execute multiple commands in sequence
  fake-camera         Inject fake camera from a video file
  wasm                WASM linear memory operations
  js                  Manage the per-domain JavaScript policy
  spa-info            Detect SPA router type
  spa-navigate        Navigate SPA without page reload
  sessions            List all sessions
  status              Show whether the session is running, what it is running and what blocks its tab
  doctor              Diagnose local eoka runtime state
  kill                Force-kill daemon
  update              Update this eoka binary and the installed agent skill from the latest GitHub release
  skill               Install or refresh the agent skill this binary ships
  close               Close browser and daemon
  cdp-url             Print the discovered DevTools WebSocket URL
  clone-from          Snapshot cookies and storage from a running Chrome
  help                Print this message or the help of the given subcommand(s)

=== eoka help network help
Network recording, inspection, export, and interception

Usage: eoka help network [COMMAND]

Commands:
  record     Start, stop or inspect network recording
  log        List recorded requests
  show       Show one recorded request
  har        Write recorded requests as a HAR file
  export     Export recorded requests as HAR or JSON
  wait       Wait for a matching request
  clear      Discard recorded requests
  intercept  Capture or mock requests matching a URL pattern

=== eoka help network long help
Network recording, inspection, export, and interception

Usage: eoka help network [COMMAND]

Commands:
  record     Start, stop or inspect network recording
  log        List recorded requests
  show       Show one recorded request
  har        Write recorded requests as a HAR file
  export     Export recorded requests as HAR or JSON
  wait       Wait for a matching request
  clear      Discard recorded requests
  intercept  Capture or mock requests matching a URL pattern

=== eoka help network record help
